#### 语音

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//2681aa5d-d500-4ab3-82aa-da400d481508/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_853_145_895_174.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-06T09%3A55%3A44Z%2F-1%2F%2Fd560c1d1c75e1f75febdc644890c6272c626fe27004fc94ceb4ccdfcd6bafa35" alt="Image" width="3%" /></div>


Pronunciation

#### 汉语的基本句调 Basic Intonations of Chinese Sentences

汉语的基本句调有两种：升调和降调。一般来说，疑问句读升调，陈述句读降调。例如：

There are two kinds of basic intonations in Chinese—the rising and the falling. Generally speaking, interrogative sentences have a rising intonation and declarative sentences have a falling intonation. For example:

Tā xìng Zhāng?

（1）他姓张？

Tā xìng Zhāng.

（2）他姓张。

Tā bù xỉhuan hē píjiǔ?

（3）他不喜欢喝啤酒？

Tā bù xử hạn hệ phối.

（4）他不喜欢喝啤酒。

### 汉字 Characters

#### 汉字偏旁“彳”和“攵” Chinese Radicals: “彳” and “攵”



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>偏旁 Radical</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>解释 Explanation</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>例字 Example Characters</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>彳</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>双人旁，大多与行走有关系。The radical “彳”，called the “double-person” radical, is usually related to the act of walking.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>行 xíng to walk 往 wǎng to go</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>攵</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>反文旁，大多与鞭打、敲打有关系。The radical “攵” is usually related to the act of whipping or beating.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>放 fàng to let go, to set free 收 shōu to receive, to accept</td></tr></table>

### 运用 Application

#### 双人活动 Pair Work

两人一组，说说你经常去的一些地方，你是怎么去这些地方的。

Work in pairs. Talk about the places where you often go and how you go there.

例如：机场 离我家 非常 远，我 坐 出租车 去 机场。



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>xuéxiào 学校</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>fànguǎn 饭馆</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>shāngdiàn 商店</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>yīyuàn 医院</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>gōngsī 公司</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>jīchǎng 机场</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>fēijī 飞机</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>zìxíngchē 自行车</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>chūzūchē 出租车</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>gōnggòng 公共</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>qìchē 汽车</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>zǒu lù 走路</td></tr></table>

### 2 小组活动 Group Work

3~4人一组，互相询问各自的生日及过生日的方式，每组请一位同学报告情况。

Work in groups of 3-4. Ask about each other's birthdays and how to celebrate birthdays. Each group chooses a member to make a report.

### 1 在教室 In the classroom

Wömen xià wǔ qù kàn diàng yǐng, hǎo ma?

A: 我们 下午去看 电影，好吗？

Jīntiān xià wǔ wǒ méiyǒu shí jiān, míngtiān

B: 今天下午我没有时间，明天xiàwǔ zài qù ba.

下午再去吧。

A: 你想看什么电影？

Ràng wǒ xiǎngxiang zài gàosu nǐ.

B: 让 我想 再告诉你。

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//8321367f-060b-4b54-a5b2-2cc0cc98ece0/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_710_217_1008_409.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-06T09%3A55%3A46Z%2F-1%2F%2Fb00ec3cc65ef077d5555aaa34d559b2ba6dd56373cf82962c842b8c3c88e08d8" alt="Image" width="26%" /></div>


##### English Version

A: Let's go to see a movie this afternoon, shall we?

##### New Words

1. 再 zài adv. again, once more

B: I'm not free this afternoon. Let's go tomorrow afternoon.

2. 让 ràng v. to let, to allow

A: Which movie would you like to see?

3. 告诉 gàosù v. to tell

B: Let me think about it and I'll tell you later.

B: 你等等我，好吗？王老师让我给大卫 $ dǎ $ ge diànhuà.

打个电话。

to wait, to await

### 1 疑问句 “……，好吗” The Interrogative Sentence “……，好吗”

常用来表示询问别人的意见和看法。例如：

It is used to ask about another person's idea or opinion. For example:

（1）我们一起去吃饭，好吗？

（2）你明天下午给我打电话，好吗？

（3）我们下午去看电影，好吗？

### 2 副词“再” The Adverb“再”

表示一个动作或一种状态重复或继续，也可用来表示一个动作将要在某一情况下出现。例如：

It indicates the repetition or continuation of an action or a state. It can also indicate that an action will happen under a certain circumstance. For example:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Subject</td><td colspan="2">Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>再</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>V (+O)</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>你</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>再</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>看看这本书吧。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>你</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>（明天）再</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>给我打电话吧。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>（你）</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>（让我想想）再</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>告诉你。</td></tr></table>

### 3 兼语句 Pivotal Sentences

兼语句的谓语是由两个动词短语组成，前一个动词的宾语是第二个动词的主语。前一个动词常常是“请、让、叫”等词语。例如：

The predicate of a pivotal sentence is made up of two verbal phrases, the object of the first verb being the subject of the second. The first verb is often a causative verb, such as “请”(to invite), “让”(to let) and “叫”(to ask). For example:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Subject</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>V</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>O/S</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>请</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>你</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>吃饭。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>你</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>让</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>再想想。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>叫</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>人</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>去看看。</td></tr></table>

### 4 动词的重叠 Reduplication of Verbs

动词的重叠形式用来表达短时间、少量、轻微、尝试的意思，语气比较轻松、随便，多用于口语中。

The reduplicative form of a verb indicates a short time, a small quantity, a slight degree or an attempt, conveying a relaxed and casual mood. It is often used in spoken Chinese.

单音节动词的重叠形式：Reduplicative forms of monosyllabic verbs:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>A</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>AA</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>A-A</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>说</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>说说</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>说一说</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>听</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>听听</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>听一听</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>看</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>看看</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>看一看</td></tr></table>

双音节动词的重叠形式：Reduplicative forms of disyllabic verbs:

#### 陈述句的句调 Intonation of a Declarative Sentence 08-5

汉语的陈述句句调一般为降调。例如：

Declarative sentences in Chinese usually have a falling intonation. For example:

Wǒ xué xí Hànyǔ.

(1) 我 学习 汉语。

Tā shì wǒ de lǎoshī.

(2) 他是我的老师。

Wàibian tiānqì hěn hǎo.

（3）外边 天气 很好。

Notes

### 1 结果补语 Complements of Result

一些动词或形容词可以放在动词后边，补充、说明动作的结果，它们叫作结果补语。例如：

Some verbs or adjectives can be used after a verb to add remarks about the result of an action. They are called complements of result. For example:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Subject</td><td colspan="2">Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>V + Complement of result</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Object</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>看 见</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>你的女朋友了。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>听 懂</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>今天的汉语课了。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>做 好</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>饭了。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>大卫</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>找 到</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>工作了。</td></tr></table>

<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">在结果补语前加 “没（有）” 表示否定，句尾不能用 “了”。例如：</div> </div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">“没（有）” is added before the verb to form the negative form of a complement of result, in which case “了” cannot appear at the end of the sentence. For example:</div> </div>




<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Subject</td><td colspan="2">Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>没（有）+ V + Complement of result</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Object</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>没有 看 见</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>你的女朋友。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>没 听 懂</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>他说的话。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>没 做 完</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>（考试题）。</td></tr></table>

表示疑问时，常在句尾加上“（了）没有”。例如：

To form a question, “（了）没有” is often added at the end of the sentence. For example:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Subject</td><td colspan="2">Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>V + Complement of result</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Object + （了）没有？</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>你</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>看 见</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我的女朋友了没有？</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>你</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>听 懂</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>他说的话没有？</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>你</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>（都）做 完</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>（考试题）了没有？</td></tr></table>

### 2 介词“从” The Preposition“从”

介词 “从” 引出一段时间、一段路程、一件事情的经过或者一个序列的起点，后面常跟 “到” 一起搭配使用。例如：

The preposition “从” introduces the starting point of a period of time, a distance, a process or a sequence, often used together with “到”. For example:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>从</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>A</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>到</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>B</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>......</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>从</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>北京</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>到</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>上海</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>要坐几个小时的飞机？</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>从</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>老人</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>到</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>孩子</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>都喜欢吃苹果。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>从</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>下个星期一</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>开始（上班）。</td></tr></table>

### 3 “第～” 表示顺序 “第～” Indicating Order

“第”常被放在数量短语前边，表示顺序。例如：

“第” is often used before a numeral-measure-word phrase to indicate order. For example:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>第</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>数词（Num）</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>量词（M）</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>名词（N）</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>第</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>一</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>本</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>书</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>第</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>二</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>个</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>工作</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>第</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>一</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>次</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>跳舞</td></tr></table>

### 语音 Pronunciation

#### 是非疑问句的句调 Intonation of a Yes-No Question 09-5

谓语部分重读，全句末为升调。

The predicate is stressed, and the end of the sentence has a rising intonation.

Nǐ míngtiān qù xuéxiào ma?

(1)你明天 $ \underset{\cdot}{去} $学校吗？

Tāmén dōu zhīdào zhè jiàn shì ma?

(2) 他们都  $ \underset{\cdot}{知}\underset{\cdot}{道} $ 这件事吗？

Nǐ dōu tīngdǒng le ma?

(3)你都  $ \underset{\cdot}{听}\underset{\cdot}{懂} $ 了吗？

### 1 在家里 At home 10-1

Búyào kàn diànshì le, míngtiān shàngwǔ

A: 不要看电视了，明天上午 hái yǒu Hàn yǔ kè ne.

还有汉语课呢。

Kàn diàn shì duì xué Hàn yǔ yǒu bāng zhù.

B: 看电视对学汉语有帮助。

Míngtiān de kè nǐ dōu zhǔnbèi hǎo le ma?

A: 明天的课你都准备好了吗？

Dōu zhǔnbèi hǎo le.

B: 都准备好了。

Nữ zài zhǎo shénme?

Nǐ kànjiàn wǒ de shǒu jī le ma? Báisè de.

Bié zhǎo le, shǒujī zài zhuōzi shang ne,

A: 别找了，手机在桌子上呢，diànǎo pángbiān.

电脑 旁边。

English Version

Notes

### 1 祈使句：不要……了；别……了

The Imperative Sentence “不要……了/别……了”

表示劝阻或禁止做某件事情。例如：

This sentence structure is used to dissuade or forbid somebody from doing something. For example:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>不要</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>V(+O)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>了</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>不要</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>玩手机</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>了。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>不要</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>做饭</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>了。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>不要</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>看电视</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>了。</td></tr></table>



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>别</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>V(+O)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>了</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>别</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>睡觉</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>了。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>别</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>看书</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>了。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>别</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>看报纸</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>了。</td></tr></table>

### 2 介词“对” The Preposition “对”

介词“对”可以表示人和人、人和事物、事物和事物之间的对待关系。例如：

<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">The preposition “对” can indicate a subject-target relation between people or things. For example:</div> </div>




<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Subject</td><td colspan="3">Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>对</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>O</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>V/Adj</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>跑步</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>对</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>身体</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>很好。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>老师</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>对</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>学生</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>非常好。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>看电视</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>对</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>学汉语</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>有帮助。</td></tr></table>

### 语音 Pronunciation

#### 特指问句的句调 Intonation of a Specific Question 10-5

疑问代词重读，重音之后全句句调逐渐下降。例如：

The interrogative pronoun is stressed, and the intonation of the part of the sentence following the stress falls gradually. For example:

Zhè shì shéi de bǐ?

(1)这是 $ \underset{\cdot}{谁} $的笔？

Nǐmen xuéxiào yǒu duōshao xuésheng?

(2) 你们 学校 有  $ \underset{\cdot}{多}\underset{\cdot}{少} $ 学生？

Nǐ zài zhǎo shénme?

(3)你在找  $ \underset{\cdot}{什}\underset{\cdot}{么} $ ?

#### 汉字

#### 汉字偏旁“走”和“穴” Chinese Radicals: “走” and “穴”

#### Characters

#### 运用 1 双人活动 Pair Work

#### Application

两人一组，选择下列词语，用 “不要……了” “别……了”练习说句子。

Work in pairs. Choose appropriate words and use "不要……了" "别……了" to make sentences.

Búyào wán rì diàn nǎo le.

例如：不要玩儿(to play)电脑了。

### 2 小组活动 Group Work

3~4人一组，用介词“对”练习说句子，每组请一位同学做记录。

Work in groups of 3-4. Practice saying sentences with the preposition “对”. Each group chooses a member to take notes.

##### 中国的茶文化 Chinese Tea Culture

中国人喜欢喝茶。茶不仅好喝，而且对身体有好处。它可以提神醒脑、抵抗衰老、预防疾病，还有减肥的功效。中国茶有很多种，比如，红茶、绿茶、青茶、花茶等。随着季节的不同，人们会选择喝不同的茶。一般来说，春天的时候喝花茶，夏天的时候喝绿茶，秋天的时候喝青茶，冬天的时候喝红茶。

如果你有机会来中国，一定要体验一下中国的茶文化。

Chinese people love drinking tea. Tea is both delicious and beneficial to our health. It can help people refresh themselves, defer aging, prevent diseases and even lose weight. There are a variety of Chinese tea, such as black tea, non-fermented green tea, half-fermented green tea and scented tea. People drink different kinds of tea in different seasons. Generally speaking, people drink scented tea in spring, non-fermented green tea in summer, half-fermented green tea in autumn, and black tea in winter.

If you have an opportunity to visit China some day, you should have a taste of the Chinese tea culture.

### 1 动词结构做定语 A Verb (Phrase) Used as an Attributive Modifier

<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">动词或动词短语做定语时，定语和中心语之间要加“的”。例如：</div> </div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">When a verb or verb phrase is used as an attributive modifier, “的” should be put between the modifier and the word modified. For example:</div> </div>




<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>定语（Attributive Modifier）</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>的</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>中心语（Word Modified）</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>新买</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>的</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>自行车</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我妈妈做</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>的</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>饭</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>和你一起唱歌</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>的</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>人</td></tr></table>

### 2 “比”字句（1） The “比” Sentence (1)

用 “比” 表示比较的句子叫 “比” 字句。“比” 字句的谓语可以是形容词。例如：

<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">A “比” sentence is a sentence using “比” to make a comparison. The predicate of the sentence can be an adjective. For example:</div> </div>




<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>A</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>比</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>B</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Adj</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>哥哥</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>比</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>姐姐</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>高。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>今天</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>比</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>昨天</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>热。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>(今天的西瓜)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>比</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>昨天</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>便宜。</td></tr></table>

<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">“比”字句的否定形式可以用“A没有B……”表示。例如：</div> </div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">“A没有B……” is the negative form of a “比” sentence. For example:</div> </div>




<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>A</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>没有</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>B</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Adj</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>哥哥</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>没有</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>姐姐</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>高。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>今天</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>没有</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>昨天</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>热。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>西瓜</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>没有</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>苹果</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>便宜。</td></tr></table>

要表达事物之间的差别时，用具体数量表示具体差别，用“一点儿”“一些”表示差别不大，用“多”“得多”表示差别大。例如：

When describing the difference between things, a specific number is used to specify the difference, “一点儿” or “一些” indicates the difference is slight, and “多” or “得多” indicates the difference is significant. For example:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>A</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>比</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>B</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Adj</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>数量短语（Num-M）</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>西瓜</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>比</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>苹果</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>贵</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>两块钱。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我的学习</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>比</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>他</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>好</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>一点儿。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>今天</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>比</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>昨天</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>热</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>得多。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>她</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>比</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我们老师</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>小</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>两岁。</td></tr></table>

### 3 助动词“可能” The Auxiliary Verb“可能”

“可能”表示估计、也许、或许。常用在动词前，也可用在主语前。例如：

It means “maybe” indicating an estimation. It can be used before the verb or subject of a sentence. For example:

（1）他可能早就知道这件事情了。

（2）可能我明天不来上课了。

（3）你可能不认识她。

### 语音 Pronunciation

#### 正反问句的句调 Intonation of an Affirmative-Negative Question 11-5

句中肯定部分重读，否定部分轻读，肯定否定重叠部分语速较快，重音后语调逐渐下降。

The affirmative part is stressed and the negative part is unstressed. The affirmative-negative phrase is read fast, and the intonation falls gradually after the stress.

Míngtiān nǐ qù bu qu $ \underset{\cdot}{ù} $ yínháng?

(1)明天 你  $ \underset{\cdot}{去} $ 不去 银行?  $ \underset{\cdot}{去} $ 不去

Zhe jian yifu nǐ xǐhuan bu xǐhuan?

(2) 这 件衣服你 $ \underset{\cdot}{喜}\underset{\cdot}{欢} $不喜欢？

Shíyī diǎn duō le, nǐ shuì bu shuì jiào?

（3）十一点多了，你睡不睡觉？

### 课文 Text

### 1 在教室 In the classroom 12-1

Nữ měi tiān zǎoshang jǐ diǎn qǐ chuáng?

A: 你每天早上几点起床？

Liù diǎn duō.

B: 六点多。

Nữ bỉ wồ zào qị yí ge xiǎoshí.

A: 你比我早起一个小时。

Wǒ shuì de yě zǎo, wǒ měi tiān wǎn shàng shí diǎn

B: 我睡得也早，我每天晚上十点jiù shuì jiào. Zǎo shuì zǎo qǐ shēntǐ hǎo. 就睡觉。早睡早起身体好。

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//85a9de22-1a48-489f-ad1b-d2e57528610f/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_733_201_1042_403.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-06T09%3A58%3A03Z%2F-1%2F%2F5bdff082207c5c627c4caeee5bbb563a7bb78d6f697a61e195070eebdc1f9843" alt="Image" width="27%" /></div>


##### English Version

A: When do you get up every morning?

B: A few minutes past six.

A: You get up one hour earlier than I do.

##### New Word

B: I go to bed early also, at 10 o'clock every night. It's healthy to keep early hours.

1. 得 de part. used after a verb or an adjective to introduce a complement of result or degree

### 2 在朋友家 At a friend's home 12-2

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//85a9de22-1a48-489f-ad1b-d2e57528610f/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_258_970_432_1123.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-06T09%3A58%3A03Z%2F-1%2F%2Fa6c89fcf6afc94e1523e5b4879f4e4f9a36b366873f743d470594235a3863c69" alt="Image" width="15%" /></div>


Zài lái diễn rõ mịtàn ba, nỉ chĩ de tài shǎo le.

A: 再来点儿米饭吧，你吃得太少了。

Bù shǎo le, jīntiān chī de hěn hǎo, tài xiè xiě nǐ le.

B: 不少了，今天吃得很好，太谢谢你了。

Nǐ zuò fàn zuò de zěnme yàng?

A: 你做饭做得怎么样?

Bù zěnme yàng, wǒ qīzi bǐ wǒ zuò de hǎo.

B: 不怎么样，我妻子比我做得好。

English Version

New Word

A: Have more rice, please. You ate too little.

2. 妻子 qīzi n. wife

B: Not a little actually. I've enjoyed the meal a lot.

### 1 程度补语 Complements of Degree

程度补语是用来表示程度的，在形式上常用结构助词 “得” 连接程度补语，补充、说明动作进行的程度或事物性质、状态所达到的程度。也有不用 “得” 连接的情况。例如：

<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">A complement of degree describes the degree of something. The structural particle “得” is often used to introduce a complement of degree which makes remarks on the extent or degree an action or the quality or state of something has reached. Sometimes “得” is not used. For example:</div> </div>




<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Subject</td><td colspan="3">Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>V</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>得</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Adj</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>他</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>说</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>得</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>很好。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>起</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>得</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>很早。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>睡</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>得</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>也早。</td></tr></table>

如果有宾语时，要把宾语提前，或者重复动词。例如：

If the verb takes an object, the object should be put before the verb, or the verb be reduplicated. For example:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Subject</td><td colspan="4">Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>(V+) O</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>V</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>得</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Adj</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>他</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>（说）汉语</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>说</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>得</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>很好。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>（写）汉字</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>写</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>得</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>很好。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>姐姐</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>（唱）歌</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>唱</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>得</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>不错。</td></tr></table>

表示否定时，要把否定词放在结构助词“得”的后边。例如：

In the negative form, the negative word should be put after the structural particle "得". For example:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Subject</td><td colspan="3">Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>V</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>得</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>不 + Adj</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>他</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>说</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>得</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>不好。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>起</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>得</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>不早。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>住</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>得</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>不远。</td></tr></table>



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Subject</td><td colspan="4">Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>(V+) O</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>V</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>得</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>不+Adj</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>他</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>（说）汉语</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>说</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>得</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>不好。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>（写）汉字</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>写</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>得</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>不好。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>姐姐</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>（唱）歌</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>唱</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>得</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>不太好。</td></tr></table>

程度补语的疑问形式是在结构助词 “得” 的后面使用 “Adj + 不 + Adj”，构成正反疑问句。例如：

In the interrogative form, “得” is followed by the structure “Adj + 不 + Adj”, forming an affirmative-negative sentence. For example:

### 语音 Pronunciation

#### 选择问句的句调 Intonation of an Alternative Question 12-5

句中供选择的部分重读，前一部分读升调，语速较慢，后一部分读降调。例如：

The alternatives are stressed, the former is a bit slow in a rising intonation and the latter in a falling intonation. For example:

Nữ xĩhuan chỉ miễn hãn chỉ miễn hãn?

(1) 你喜欢吃 $ \underset{\cdot}{米}\underset{\cdot}{饭} $还是吃 $ \underset{\cdot}{面}\underset{\cdot}{条} $?

Nǐ xiǎng jīntiān qù háishi míngtiān qù?

(2) 你想  $ \underset{\cdot}{今}\underset{\cdot}{天} $ 去还是  $ \underset{\cdot}{明}\underset{\cdot}{天} $ 去？

Nǐ qù xuéxiào shì kāi chē háishi zuò chē？

(3) 你去 学校 是  $ \underset{\cdot}{开}\underset{\cdot}{车} $ 还是  $ \underset{\cdot}{坐}\underset{\cdot}{车} $?

#### 运用 1 双人活动 Pair Work

### 运用 Application

两人一组，用带助词“得”的程度补语练习说句子，尽量使用所给出的词语。

Work in pairs and make sentences with complements of degree using “得”. Use as many given words as possible.

Tā pǎo de hěn kuài.

Wỏ pǎo de bú kuài.

例如：他跑得很快。

我跑得不快。

### 2 小组活动 Group Work

4~5人一组，进行“比”字句接龙的游戏。要求使用“A比B+V+得+Adj”或“A+V+得+比B+Adj”两种句型。

Work in groups of 4-5 and say sentences with “比” in relays. Use either “A比B + V + 得 + Adj” or “A + V + 得 + 比B + Adj”.

Wỏ bỉ Dày (đọc là Dày) bằng de kuài.

例如：王方：我比大卫跑得快。

Wỏ chàng de bỉ  $ \underline{\text{An}} $nhi hảo.

大卫：我唱得比安妮好。

热身

Warm-up

### 课文 Text

### 1 在办公室 In the office 13-1

Mén kãi zhe ne, qăng jìn.

A: 门开着呢，请进。

Qǐngwèn, Zhāng xiānsheng zài ma?

B: 请问，张先生在吗？

Tā chūqu le. Nǐ xiàwǔ zài lái ba.

A: 他出去了。你下午再来吧。

Hǎo de, xièxié!

B: 好的，谢谢！

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//0f9eeb50-b30b-46f2-a115-552a6294a42b/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_666_199_979_405.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-06T09%3A54%3A51Z%2F-1%2F%2F20d75b27d44a7ef8cd72e1c8c6e773cb32863b11e21c2e25fc59666d95e81dbb" alt="Image" width="27%" /></div>


##### English Version

A: The door is open. Come on in.

B: Excuse me, is Mr. Zhang in?

##### New Word

A: He's gone out. Please come in the afternoon.

B: OK. Thank you!

1. 着 zhe part. used to indicate a state

### 2 在办公室 In the office 13-2

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//0f9eeb50-b30b-46f2-a115-552a6294a42b/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_247_826_484_987.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-06T09%3A54%3A51Z%2F-1%2F%2Fbbf52185fb5b69b1a062f20265df3246f1882fdfc7496f9bbb1f8ecba783aafd" alt="Image" width="21%" /></div>


Nàge zhèngzài shuōhuà de nǚháir shì shéi?

A: 那个正在说话的女孩儿是谁?

Wǒ zhīdào tā de míngzi, tā xìng Yáng, jiào Yáng Xiàoxiao,

B: 我知道她的名字，她姓杨，叫杨笑笑，tā jiějie shì wǒ tóngxué.

她姐姐是我同学。

Nàge shǒu li názhe qiānbǐ de ne?

A: 那个手里拿着铅笔的呢?

Wǒ bú rènshi.

B: 我不认识。

##### English Version

A: Who is the girl speaking?

B: I know her name. Her name is Yang Xiaoxiao. Yang is her family name. Her elder sister is my classmate.

A: What about the girl with a pencil in her hand?

##### New Words

B: That one I don't know.

2. 手 shǒu n. hand

 $ ^{*}3. $拿 n $ \underset{\cdot}{a} $ v. to hold, to take, to bring

4. 铅笔 qiānbǐn n. pencil

##### Proper Noun

杨笑笑 Yáng Xiàoxiao name of a person

Tīngshuō nǐ yǒu nǚpéngyou le? Wǒ rènshi tā ma?

Jiù shì wǒmen bān nàge zhǎngzhe liǎng ge dà yǎnjìng,

B: 就是我们班那个长着两个大眼睛，

 $ \underline{\text{fěicháng ài xiào de nǚháir.}} $

非常爱笑的女孩儿。

Nàge yǐjīng shì tā de qián nányǒu le.

to grow, to develop

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//0f9eeb50-b30b-46f2-a115-552a6294a42b/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_202_868_469_1045.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-06T09%3A54%3A51Z%2F-1%2F%2F3aa2699f7ca7df1d5aec64aec302d4a535fa28d7999b9ebd0e8d4a2f03c9b51c" alt="Image" width="23%" /></div>


Qingwen, zhè lí Xīnjīng Bīngguǎn yuǎn ma?

A: 请问，这儿离新京宾馆远吗？

Bù yuǎn, zǒu lù èrshí fēnzhōng jiù dào.

B: 不 远，走路二十分钟 就到。

Nǐ néng gàosu wǒ zěnme zǒu ma?

A: 你能告诉我怎么走吗?

Cóng zhè yìzhí wǎng qián zǒu, dàole qiánmiàn

B: 从这儿一直往前走，到了前面的路口再往右走。

### 1 结构助词 “着” The Structural Particle “着”

动词加上结构助词 “着” 可以表示某种状态的持续。例如：

A verb followed by the structural particle “着” can indicate the continuation of a certain state. For example:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Subject</td><td colspan="3">Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>V</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>着</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>O</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>门</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>开</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>着。</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>他们</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>穿</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>着</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>红色的衣服。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>（她）</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>拿</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>着</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>铅笔。</td></tr></table>

在动词前加“没”表示否定。例如：

In the negative form, “没” is added before the verb. For example:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Subject</td><td colspan="3">Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>没+V</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>着</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>O</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>门</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>没开</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>着。</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>他们</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>没穿</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>着</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>红色的衣服。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>（她）</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>没拿</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>着</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>铅笔。</td></tr></table>

在句末加“没有”表示疑问。例如：

In the interrogative form, “没有” is added at the end of the sentence. For example:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Subject</td><td colspan="4">Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>V</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>着</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>O</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>没有</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>门</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>开</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>着</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>没有？</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>他们</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>穿</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>着</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>红色的衣服</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>没有？</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>她（手里）</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>拿</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>着</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>铅笔</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>没有？</td></tr></table>

### 2 反问句“不是……吗” The Rhetorical Question“不是……吗”

“不是……吗”常用来表示提醒或者表达说话人的不理解、不满等。例如：

“不是……吗” is often used to remind someone of something or to show confusion or dissatisfaction. For example:

（2）你不是北京人吗？怎么不会说北京话？

（1）不是说今天有雨吗？怎么没下？

（3）（她是你的女朋友？）她不是有男朋友了吗？

### 3 介词“往” The Preposition “往”

介词“往”常常用来指示方向。例如：

The preposition "往" is often used to indicate direction. For example:

（1）从这儿往前走，就是我们学校。

（2）你看，往左走是医院，往右走是银行。

(3) 从这儿一直 $ \underset{\cdot}{往} $前走，到了前面的路口再往右走。

语音

Pronunciation

祈使句的句调 Intonation of an Imperative Sentence 13-5

语气委婉时，全句音高较低，第一个分句末尾语调略升，全句末尾语调缓降。例如：

When the tone is polite, the whole sentence is said in a low pitch, the first clause ending in a slightly rising intonation and the whole sentence ending in a smoothly falling intonation. For example:

Ràng wômen xiū xi xiū ba.

(1) 让 我们休息休息吧。  $ \downarrow $

Kuaidiǎnr xià kè ba.

(2) 快点儿 下课吧。 $ \downarrow $

Qǐng zuò ba.

（3）请坐吧。

#### 汉字偏旁 “ $ \underset{\cdot}{斤} $” 和 “ $ \underset{\cdot}{页} $” Chinese Radicals: “ $ \underset{\cdot}{斤} $” and “ $ \underset{\cdot}{页} $”



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>偏旁 Radical</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>解释 Explanation</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>例字 Example Characters</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>斤</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>斤字旁，一般与斧头及砍削的动作有关系。 The radical &quot;斤&quot; is usually related to axes or the action of cutting or whittling.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>新 xīn new 所 suǒ place</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>页</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>页字旁，一般与人头、面部有关系。 The radical &quot;页&quot; is usually related to the human head or face.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>颜 yán face, look 须 xū beard, mustache</td></tr></table>

### 运用 Application

##### 双人活动 Pair Work

两人一组，其中一个人用“往……”说句子，另一个同学做出相应的动作，然后两个人交替进行。

Work in pairs. One says sentences using "往……", and the other acts accordingly. Then switch the roles.

例如：往前走 wǎng qián zǒu wǎng hòu pǎo 往后跑 wǎng zuǒ kàn wǎng yòu zǒu 往 左看 wǎng yòu zǒu 往 右走

### 2 小组活动 Group Work

3~4人一组，用结构助词“着”描述图片内容。每组请一位同学做记录。

Work in groups of 3-4. Describe the picture using the structural particle “着”. Each group chooses a member to take notes.

Yǒu liǎng ge xuésheng chuānzhe báisè de yīfu.

### 1 结构助词 “过” The Structural Particle “过”

动词后加上结构助词 “过”，一般用来表示过去有过的经历，这些动作行为没有持续到现在。例如：

A verb followed by the structural particle “过” usually indicates a past experience action which hasn’t lasted to the present. For example:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Subject</td><td colspan="3">Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>V</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>O</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>他们</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>来</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我家。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>看</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>那个电影。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>去</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>中国。</td></tr></table>

在动词前边加“没（有）”表示否定。例如：

In the negative form, "没（有）" is added before the verb. For example:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Subject</td><td colspan="3">Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>没（有）+V</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>O</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>他们</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>没（有）来</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我家。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>没（有）看</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>那个电影。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>没（有）去</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>中国。</td></tr></table>

在句末加“没有”表示疑问。例如：

In the interrogative form, "没有" is added at the end of the sentence. For example:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Subject</td><td colspan="3">Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>V</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>O+ 没有</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>他们</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>来</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>你家没有？</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>你</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>看</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>那个电影没有？</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>你</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>去</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>中国没有？</td></tr></table>

### 2 关联词“虽然……，但是……”

The Pair of Conjunctions “虽然……，但是……”

“虽然……，但是……”连接两个分句，构成一种转折关系。例如：

The conjunctions “虽然……，但是……” connect two clauses, forming a complex sentence indicating an adversative relation. For example:

（1）虽然外面很冷，但是房间里很热。

（2）虽然汉字很难，但是我很喜欢写汉字。

（3）虽然是晴天，但是很冷。

### 3 动量补语“次” The Complement of Frequency“次”

动量补语 “次” 常放在谓语动词的后边，用来表示动作发生、进行的次数。例如：

The complement of frequency “次” is usually used after the predicate verb, indicating the number of times that an action has taken place. For example:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Subject</td><td colspan="4">Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>V</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Num + 次</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>O</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我们</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>看</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>三次</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>电影。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>他们</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>坐</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>一次</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>飞机。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>（在这个商店）买</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>一次</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>（东西）。</td></tr></table>

宾语是表示地点的名词时，动量补语可以放在宾语前，也可以放在宾语后。例如：

When the object of a verb is a place, the complement of frequency can be put either before or after the object. For example:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Subject</td><td colspan="4">Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>V</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Num + 次</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>O</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我们</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>去</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>三次</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>北京。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>他们</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>来</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>一次</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>中国。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>（上星期）去</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>一次</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>医院。</td></tr></table>



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Subject</td><td colspan="4">Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>V</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>O</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Num + 次</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我们</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>去</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>北京</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>三次。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>他们</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>来</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>中国</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>一次。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>（上星期）去</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>医院</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>一次。</td></tr></table>

宾语是人称代词时，动量补语要放在宾语后。例如：

When the object is a personal pronoun, the complement of frequency should be put after the object. For example:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Subject</td><td colspan="4">Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>V</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>O</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Num+次</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我们</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>找</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>他</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>三次。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>他们</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>看</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>一次。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>老师</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>叫</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>过</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>我</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>两次。</td></tr></table>

1分角色朗读课文 Role-play the dialogs.

2 根据课文内容回答问题 Answer the questions based on the dialogs.

① 他们看过那个电影吗？Tāmen kànguo nàge diàn yǐng ma?

②他们想几个人去看电影？Tāmen xiǎng jǐ ge rén qù kàn diàn yǐng？

③他们想来中国做什么？Tāmen xiǎng lái Zhōngguó zuò shénme？

④为什么她明天不能去跑步？Wèi shénme tā míngtiān bù néng qù pǎo bù？

⑤ 女的觉得这个商店的东西怎么样？

Nüde juéde zhège shāngdiàn de dōngxi zěnmeyàng?

#### 感叹句的句调 Intonation of an Exclamatory Sentence

汉语感叹句的句调一般为降调。例如：

A Chinese exclamatory sentence usually has a falling intonation. For example:

Jintian tiānqì zhēn hǎo a!

(1) 今天 天气 真 好啊！

Zhège Hànzì zhēn nán xiě a!

(2) 这个汉字真难写啊！

Zhe jian yīfu tài piàoliang le!

（3）这件衣服太漂亮了！

### 汉字 Characters

#### 汉字偏旁 “耒” 和 “贝” Chinese Radicals: “耒” and “贝”



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>偏旁 Radical</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>解释 Explanation</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>例字 Example Characters</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>雨</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>雨字头，一般与云、雨等天气现象有关系。 The radical “雨”，appearing at the top of a character, is usually related to such weather phenomena as clouds and rain.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>雪 xuě snow 雾 wù fog, mist</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>贝</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>贝字旁，一般与钱财、鼎类器物有关系。 The radical “贝” is usually related to money or utensils like an ancient cooking vessel.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>财 cái wealth, money 货 huò goods, commodity</td></tr></table>

### 运用 Application

### 1 双人活动 Pair Work

两人一组，用 “虽然A，但是B” 练习说句子，其中一个人说A，另一个人说B。然后两人互换。

Work in pairs and make sentences using “虽然A，但是B”. One says A and the other says B. Then switch the roles.

Suī rán zhè jiàn yī fu hěn piàoliang,

例如：A：虽然 这件衣服很 漂亮，

dành tài quì le, wỏ méi qián mǎi.

B：但是太贵了，我没钱买。

Suī rán jīntiān tiānqì hěn lěng,

A：虽然 今天 天气 很 冷，

B: ___

### 2 小组活动 Group Work

3~4人一组，用结构助词“过”叙述自己在中国经历过的事情，尽量使用学过的词语。每组请一位同学报告情况。

Work in groups of 3-4. Describe your experiences in China using the structural particle “过” with the words you’ve learned. Each group chooses a member to make a report.

课文

Text

15-1

### 1 在朋友家 At a friend's home

Jintian shì shí'er yuè ěrshí rì, xīnnián jiù yào dào le.

A: 今天是12月20日，新年就要到了。

Xīnnián nǐ zhǔnbèi zuò shénme?

B: 新年你准备做什么?

Wǒ xiǎng qù Běijīng lǚyóu, Běijīng hěn búcuò,

A: 我想去北京旅游，北京很不错，wǒ qùguo yí cì.

我去过一次。

Nǐ mǎi piào le ma?

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//1d9fb034-008b-46ef-988f-054b7881004f/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_773_194_1044_373.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-06T09%3A57%3A58Z%2F-1%2F%2F8af15f8ad79354bcbc4ab2d839788710d6567ce880456dda2f75f127857ffaac" alt="Image" width="24%" /></div>


B: 你买票了吗?

Hái méiyǒu ne, míngtiān jiù qù huǒchēzhàn mǎi piào.

A: 还没有呢，明天就去火车站买票。

##### English Version

A: Today is December 20th. The New Year is coming.

B: What plans do you have?

A: I want to take a trip to Beijing. Beijing is a nice place. I've been there once.

B: Have you bought the ticket?

A: Not yet. I'll go to the railway station to buy it tomorrow.

##### New Words

1. 日 rì n. day, date

2. 新年 xīnnián n. New Year

3. 票 piào n. ticket

4. 火车站 huǒchēzhàn n. railway station

### 1 动作的状态 “要……了” “要……了” Indicating the State of an Action

用 “快要/快/就要/要……了” 表示某事将要发生。例如：

The structure “快要/快/就要/要……了” indicates that something is going to happen. For example:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Subject</td><td colspan="3">Predicate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>快要/快/就要/要</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>V (+ O)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>了</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>火车</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>快要</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>来</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>了。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>要</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>下雨</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>了。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>新的一年</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>快要</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>到</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>了。</td></tr></table>

如果句子中有时间状语，只能用“就要……了”。例如：

If there is an adverbial of time in the sentence, then only “就要……了” can be used. For example:

### 2 “都……了” The Structure “都……了”

“都……了”可以表示“已经”的意思，通常含有强调或不满的语气。例如：

The structure “都……了” means “already”, usually conveying an emphatic or a complaining tone. For example:

（1）都8点了，快点儿起床吧。

（2）你都十岁了，可以自己洗衣服了。

（3）都等她半个小时了。

语音

Pronunciation

#### 用 “吧” 和 “吗” 构成的疑问句的句调 15-5

Intonation of a Question Ending with “吧” or “吗”

用 “吧” 构成的疑问句的句调为降调，用 “吗” 构成的疑问句的句调为升调。例如：

A question ending with “吧” has a falling intonation, and one that ends with “吗” has a rising intonation. For example:

Zhè běn shū shì nǐ de ba?

(1) 这本书是你的吧？

Míngtiān shì xīngqī èr ba?

(2) 明天 是 星期二 吧?  $ \downarrow $

Nǐ mǎi piào le ba?

（3）你买票了吧？

Zhè běn shū shì nǐ de ma?

这本书是你的吗？

Míngtiān shì xīngqī èr ma?

明天是星期二吗？

Nǐ mǎi piào le ma?

你买票了吗？

### 2 小组活动 Group Work

3~4人一组，说说你自己或者你的家人、朋友们在以后的几个月里学习上、生活上、工作上都有哪些计划或者变化。用“快要/快/就要/要……了”进行表述。每组请一位同学做记录。

Work in groups of 3-4. Talk about the plans or changes that you, your family members or your friends have or will have regarding study, life or work during the next few months. Use the structure “快要/快/就要/要……了”. Each group chooses a member to take notes.

#### 文化 CULTURE

#### 中国的“新年”——春节 The Chinese New Year—Spring Festival

春节是中国人的“新年”，是中国最重要的传统节日。春节是个欢乐祥和的节日，也是亲人团聚的日子。每年春节的时候，离家在外的人们都会赶回家中，和亲人一起过春节。大家一起贴对联、放鞭炮、吃饺子、看春节联欢晚会，高高兴兴地过年。孩子们最喜欢过春节了，因为他们可以从长辈那里得到压岁钱。节日的热烈气氛不仅洋溢在各家各户，也充满大街小巷。一些地方还会有舞狮子、耍龙灯、逛庙会等文化活动。

Spring Festival, or the Chinese New Year, is the most important traditional festival in China. It is a happy, merry day when family members have a reunion. Every year during the Spring Festival, people away from home will return home to celebrate the festival with their families. Together they paste antithetical couplets, light off firecrackers, some eat dumplings, and watch the Spring Festival Gala happily and cheerfully. Kids love the Spring Festival best because they can get money from their elders as gifts. The bustling, festive ambience fills not only every household, but also the streets and lanes. In some places, there are such cultural activities as lion dances, dragon-lantern shows and temple fairs, etc.
